# Doing: Spoonjoy Cross-Client Delivery

**Status**: NEEDS_REVIEW
**Execution Mode**: direct
**Created**: 2026-07-20 21:27
**Planning**: ./2026-07-20-1958-planning-cross-client-delivery.md
**Artifacts**: ./2026-07-20-1958-doing-cross-client-delivery/

## Execution Mode

- **direct**: Execute units sequentially in the coordinating task. Fresh sub-agents perform the mandatory hostile reviews. Before protected handoff, this task may create and protect only `spoonjoy-delivery` plus dedicated delivery-control-plane GitHub App identities and exact root-reviewed disabled `BrokerFoundationV1` Cloudflare resources; namespace existence alone grants no import or operation authority. It cannot target or consume source-owned web/native, production Cloudflare, ASC, TestFlight, notification, build, secret, or worktree state. Source-repository work begins only after the retained TestFlight task's protected handoff validates.
- **public evidence**: Every repository artifact/output uses repository, random 128-bit stable worktree ID, role, branch, and base SHA only. No local path or unkeyed path-derived digest is public. ID-to-path mappings and optional keyed HMAC integrity commitments exist solely in private Desk cleanup state and are never committed, uploaded, logged, summarized, or embedded through another object. Until Unit 6c is complete, every command transcript and hostile fixture result remains outside the repository in `0600` private ephemeral storage. A generated `PreGateTranscriptManifestV1` keys every code/test unit preceding Unit 6c in this document's exact ordered unit index to its private Desk locator and SHA-256 digest; those units commit code/tests only. Unit 6c scans every exact manifest entry and may then commit only allowlisted summaries/digests; raw bytes are purged after the backfill proof.

## Objective

Build and pilot a small, production-grade delivery system that carries one Spoonjoy product contract through web/backend, native Apple, and MCP/agent implementations to independently verified exact releases and terminal cleanup. Only a provider-backed Release Set may declare a Product Change shipped.

## Upstream Work Items

- Codex task `019f2e25-2fc3-75b2-8ba3-335f3777115a`: its protected source-owner handoff and byte-identical receiver acknowledgment are prerequisites for Unit 13b; it retains exclusive native/TestFlight/provider-release ownership until terminal two-sided validation succeeds.

## Completion Criteria

- [ ] spoonjoy-delivery is protected, reproducible, strictly typed, warning-free, and green at 100% statements, branches, functions, and lines.
- [ ] Versioned parsers and semantic validators fail closed for malformed structure, illegal transitions, stale or ambiguous evidence, invalid authority, dependency cycles, and incomplete cleanup.
- [ ] Product Contracts express normative behavior, compatibility, ownership, failure, recovery, migration, rollback, and cross-surface scenarios; semantic, pack, and provenance identities have tested non-overlapping meanings.
- [ ] Web/backend emits the reviewed Contract Pack and runtime identity; native locks and embeds the expected identities and produces signed external build provenance.
- [ ] No source or provider release work begins until byte-identical protected acknowledgments on delivery, web, and native plus the protected ledger event validate the retained owner's exact handoff and cleanup ownership.
- [ ] Every review, ledger, environment, provider-read, and provider-mutation authority is least-privilege, identity-bound, replay-resistant, independently queryable, and unable to exercise adjacent roles.
- [ ] Environment-gated jobs use GitHub's documented `environment: {name, deployment: false}` form so required reviewers and wait timers still apply, custom deployment protection rules are prohibited as incompatible, and no GitHub Deployment/status object is created.
- [ ] Every provider mutation is covered by a reviewed typed operation graph, authoritative pre/post queries, idempotent receipt, crash/response-loss recovery, and rollback or containment.
- [ ] Source-owned attestors bind exact repository/ref/workflow/run/job/environment and provider evidence while keeping raw secrets and private evidence outside public artifacts.
- [ ] Finalization cannot strand or split ownership after coordinator, observer, broker, workflow, or response failure; exact-candidate publication remains relay-only.
- [ ] PublicationObserved is an independently generated non-ledger Guardian check/artifact for the exact published result; shipment cannot become effective without it and terminal recovery cleanup.
- [ ] Required web, native, MCP/protocol, and agent-experience surfaces prove the same Photo Studio semantics with explicitly classified required, no_op, or non-shipping dispositions.
- [ ] Current/previous-client compatibility, offline queues, installed TestFlight behavior, accessibility, and visual dogfood are proven wherever the surface is required; unavailable physical proof is explicit and non-shipping.
- [ ] The final Release Set is compiled from fresh authoritative provider evidence, exact protected source/build identities, effective publication observation, and complete cleanup, then projected without creating an alternate shipment authority.
- [ ] All run-owned rows, objects, clients, media, branches, artifacts, capabilities, temporary services, and worktrees reach verified terminal cleanup or an explicit retained-provider disposition; unrelated state is untouched.

## Code Coverage Requirements

**MANDATORY: 100% coverage on all new code.**
- No coverage exclusions or warning suppression on new delivery, web, workflow-support, or native core logic.
- Every statement, branch, function, line, error path, retry, parser failure, transition, race, predicate alternative, redaction, provider failure, rollback, supersession, and cleanup refusal is tested.
- Edge cases include null, empty, duplicate, Unicode, numeric boundary, expiry boundary, malformed SHA/digest/ID, unknown field/type, ambiguous predicate, stale read, concurrent append, partial provider failure, and leaked evidence.
- Full repository suites and changed-code coverage remain green with zero warnings before each merge.

## TDD Requirements

**Strict TDD - no exceptions:**
1. **Tests first**: Write failing tests before implementation.
2. **Verify failure**: Run the focused command and capture the expected red result in private `0600` ephemeral storage. Before Unit 6c, commit no command transcript or fixture output; after Unit 6c, publish only scanner-approved summaries/digests.
3. **Minimal implementation**: Write only enough code to satisfy the contract.
4. **Verify pass**: Re-run focused tests and capture green evidence under the same private-before-Unit-6c/public-after-scan rule.
5. **Refactor**: Keep behavior and coverage green.
6. **No skipping**: Workflow, settings, shell, Swift, and live-provider behavior require executable contract tests, not prose assertions.

### ⬜ Unit 2s10a: Replay Execution Schemas - Tests
**What**: Add closed `ReplayPassV1` states `staged | open | draining | failed | checkpointed | repair_completed | resolved | superseded`, node states `unclaimed | claimed | recovering | terminal | consumed | blocked_by_predecessor`, and original-ID-keyed `ReplayManifestCursorV1` union `at(nodeID) | complete(manifestDigest) | failed(causalNodeID,failureDigest)`. Add root-policy-governed `ReplayPassGenesisV1` for the sole first activation: the current protected Product Change graph record binds its concrete `ReplayNodeSetV1` and `ReplayNodeIdentityV1`; active pointer is absent, pass history is empty, `repair_iteration=i1`, `passNumber=1`, and one atomic candidate/node creation, cursor-at-first-node installation, pointer installation, and `staged -> open` occurs. Reject a second genesis, prior history, pointer mismatch, alternate Product Change/graph/manifest, partial commit, or non-p1 activation, and reconcile lost responses by exact pointer/pass/cursor bytes. Later activation atomically supersedes only `resolved | repair_completed`. Every claim CAS requires cursor `at` that exact original ID, a consumed ordered prefix, every successor unclaimed, and zero other claimed/recovering/terminal node; out-of-order or concurrent claims fail. Exact node transitions are `unclaimed -> claimed`, `claimed -> terminal` on exact success/containment, `claimed -> recovering` on expired-lease fencing, `recovering -> unclaimed` with attempt+1 after authoritative no-call, and `recovering -> terminal` from exact provider success/containment; uncertainty stays `recovering`, and all other transitions fail. A green/no-replay/refresh terminal consumption CAS changes `terminal -> consumed`, advances the cursor to the exact successor or `complete`, and stores immutable `ReplayNodeConsumedV1`; response loss accepts exact immediate poststate or a validated monotonic descendant receipt chain. A containment consumption CAS atomically changes the causal `terminal -> consumed`, every successor `unclaimed -> blocked_by_predecessor`, cursor `at -> failed`, and pass `draining -> failed`, and stores exact `ReplayStageFailedV1`; there is no claimable or failure-record-free intermediate, and response loss accepts only exact full-state equality. When replay Unit 31 retained staged state, original node 32, 33, 34, or 35 cannot enter containment consumption until its node-specific cleanup and the exact generation-bound `StagedWorkerCleanupAuthorityV1` terminal are durable; cleanup response loss keeps the node recovering at the same cursor. Define complete bijective `ReplayNodeIdentityV1` entries whose canonical state key is `originalUnitID` and whose `executorUnitID` is metadata only; reject executor-keyed state/index/claim/blocked records, duplicate or missing mappings, and mixed domains. `ReplayNodeClaimV1` binds pass/original node/cursor/attempt, identities, root-bound `replayNodeLeaseDurationMs=900000`, canonical issue/deadline/renewal/fence records from exact no-cache protected-ledger-ref GitHub Dates, durable journal, idempotency, request, prestate, and graph. Generic containment consumes the causal node, blocks its full suffix, and fails cursor/pass atomically. Lease recovery preserves cursor and resolves only exact no-call, result, or continuing uncertainty. Unit 60d1c checkpoints; Unit 60c29f installs exact repair closure. **Normative Delivery CI and Settings Contract Implementation (reference only)**
**Required-check authority**: `ProtectedMainV1` does not trust a GitHub Actions check from `{context, Actions App ID}` alone. Its required contexts must be emitted by the distinct Delivery Guardian App after the Guardian broker re-queries and binds exact workflow database ID/path, protected workflow blob SHA, event, head SHA, run/attempt/job, artifact digest, check payload, and a canonical `ReviewerEnvelopeV1` signed by the root-anchored workflow-inaccessible Secure Enclave reviewer key. The envelope binds two distinct fresh reviewer task/model/verdict/evidence records, target head/tree/workflow, prompt/lens, nonce, expiry, signer generation, and revocation head; Guardian consumes its nonce exactly once. Branch rules require zero human approvals and zero bypass. `ProtectedLedgerV1` separately permits only the Ledger Writer App bypass.
**Release-tag authority**: Normalize/verify active tag rulesets independently from branch/ledger rules. `ProtectedReleaseTagV1` matches only recursive `refs/tags/release-set/**/*`, proves the canonical two-level tag is included under GitHub `FNM_PATHNAME`, requires creation/update/deletion/non-fast-forward restrictions, and exactly one always-bypass Ledger Writer Integration. Keep Administration permission away from Ledger Writer. A distinct root-authorized local governance observer uses one operator credential/session for only no-cache `GET /user`, exact collaborator-permission GET, and policy-pinned ruleset GET. It requires exact provider-returned actor/collaborator ID/login, built-in `admin` permission/role, three fresh `200`s, bound bodies/Date/request IDs, complete bypass actors, and stable ruleset ETag before signing canonical one-use `ProtectedReleaseTagSnapshotV1`; cache hits, `304`, identity/role/time drift, and any other route fail. Its broker validates `release-tag-project-v1` against fresh matching pre/post snapshots, requires the canonical annotated tag ref already exist before Release POST, creates an exact body-only empty-assets GitHub Release with fixed note/latest/discussion settings, and exposes no update/delete/force/general-tag/release-asset endpoint.
**What**: Add SHA-pinned CI/resolver. Define `ProtectedMainV1` with repository-specific `{context, expected Guardian App ID}` required checks and zero classic/ruleset bypass principals or App allowances, and `ProtectedLedgerV1` with exactly the policy-pinned Ledger Writer App bypass and no ordinary writer path; both normalize/stability-check all layers. Add strict repository-settings schemas/adapters for exact no-cache `GET/PUT /repos/spoonjoy/spoonjoy-delivery/actions/permissions/workflow` and exact no-cache `GET` plus bounded `PATCH /repos/spoonjoy/spoonjoy-delivery`; only `read`/`false` and `delete_branch_on_merge: true` are valid target states. Implement a strict semantic workflow scanner that reads every exact protected-tree workflow blob, rejects duplicate/aliased/expressive permission syntax and `pull_request_target`, resolves explicit top-level and job-level effective permissions plus every same-repository relative reusable-workflow edge from that same exact tree, rejects external/dynamic reusable-workflow calls, and emits canonical `WorkflowTokenPolicyV1` with sorted workflow/job/scope inventories, blob/reusable-closure digests, and a policy digest. Allow only explicit `read` scopes and policy-bound job-specific `id-token: write` or `attestations: write`; reject all repository-mutating writes including `actions`, `checks`, `contents`, `deployments`, `discussions`, `issues`, `packages`, `pages`, `pull-requests`, `repository-projects`, `security-events`, and `statuses`. Journal expected settings prestate, request digest, dispatch uncertainty, repeated GET reconciliation, exact post-state, rollback/containment, complete workflow scan, and root/replay binding. Implement serialized broker identity/actor/transition validation, `ReviewerEnvelopeV1` verification and atomic nonce consumption, source-cutover transition validation, plus distinct web/native envelope-key generations and one-use unwrap. Canonical recipient key is raw RFC 7748 32-byte X25519 u-coordinate in base64url without padding; fingerprint is lowercase-hex SHA-256 of those bytes. Capsule encryption is RFC 9180 base-mode HPKE suite `{KEM=X25519-HKDF-SHA256(0x0020),KDF=HKDF-SHA256(0x0001),AEAD=ChaCha20Poly1305(0x0003)}` with fresh ephemeral KEM key and sequence zero. Strict RFC 8785 envelope is exactly `{version:1,suite,enc,ciphertext,payloadDigest,context}` with 32-byte `enc` and base64url-no-padding fields. `info` is `UTF8("spoonjoy-supervisor-capsule-v1") || 0x00 || RFC8785({repository,workflow,keyGeneration})`; AAD binds owner/repository/workflow/ref/SHA/run/attempt/checkRun/job/environment/claim/generation/nonce/expiry/payloadDigest/signerPublicKey. `payloadDigest` hashes canonical plaintext; claim separately binds final-envelope SHA-256. Reject reused `enc`/nonce/context, caller AEAD nonce, and noncanonical encoding. Normal unwrap requires exact active Provisioned event whose `startedCommit`, generation, canonical key, fingerprint, challenge receipt, and prior local-zero receipt validate against Started/broker state; validate OIDC/JTI, consume the ordinary nonce only after kind/endpoint checks, return signer bytes once, and reject replay/retired/contained. Provisional endpoint requires exact Started event/claim/generation/key/fingerprint/challenge job/nonce, checks capsule kind before nonce consumption, decrypts only fixed challenge once, and cannot unwrap signer capsules. Typed wrong-kind rejection uses disjoint nonce namespaces and consumes neither the challenge nor ordinary capsule. Challenge and local-zero receipts bind start commit/key/fingerprint; only an exactly preserving Provisioned transition containing both receipts activates ordinary unwrap. Timeout/crash deletes binding and appends Aborted or contains all unwraps. App key never leaves broker; envelope private key exists first only in the isolated provisioning context, then broker storage, and local purge/fsync proof must precede Provisioned. Model append state independently from credential lease; pre-mint containment, returned expiry/fingerprint persistence, `finally` revocation `204` or expiry containment, tokenless `H`, fresh-token abort CAS, and `cas_uncertain` persistence remain mandatory.
**Output**: Reviewable CI, governance, and broker-foundation code.

**What**: Before private query, initialize enumerable `0700` root and fsynced WAL `ALLOCATING -> OPEN -> SEALED -> PURGING -> PURGED -> PROMOTED`. Independently launch a policy-pinned promotion-witness service under a third UID/session with its own ephemeral Ed25519 key, root read capability, and shared-lock capability but no provider credential, cleanup write, child-control, or supervisor-signing capability. Launch a distinct promotion-submitter service under a fourth UID/session with a separate non-exportable ephemeral key and exact HTTPS allowlist only to the Durable Object submission/status routes. Launch a fifth attempt-specific supervised root-policy-bound `FinalizationPreRegistrationRecoveryAuthorityV1` with its own `0700` fsynced plan/dispatch/cleanup/closure journal, strict protected-ledger-ref GET allowlist, exact Ledger Writer routes only for `FinalizationPreRegistrationCloseStartedV1` and `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, the sole local route to invoke the cleanup supervisor's registration-independent subset, and exact nonreachability query routes. It has no reservation/registration/source/provider credential, direct cleanup authority, claim/package bytes, general ledger method, or access to witness/submitter private keys. It remains independently restartable through plan-only cleanup or, after close-start plus zero registration/claim, cleanup invocation/reconciliation, exact closed append, complete nonreachability proof, and its own teardown. Exact registration preserves the same service identity/public key and atomically retires only pre-registration grants while installing registered strict protected-ledger-ref GET, exact parent-bound `FinalizationPreClaimCloseStartedV1`, claim-independent supervisor cleanup, receipt/nonreachability-bound exact `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, and authoritative nonreachability-query routes; claim intent, claim, direct cleanup, package/source/provider access, and general ledger remain denied. Bind all service code/policy/public-key/context and attempt/cleanup-supervisor identities into final preflight and claim before private work. Linux uses root-owned sockets with `SO_PEERCRED`, `FD_CLOEXEC`, non-dumpable/no-new-privileges children, read-only root/private tmpfs, seccomp and dedicated cgroups killed empty. macOS uses root launchd sockets with local peer credentials, newly created unprivileged UIDs, denied task-port/ptrace/privilege escalation, deny-by-default `sandbox-exec` profiles, and UID-wide process exhaustion. **What**: Exercise only witness and submitter isolation/lifecycle fixtures: unused-key proof, wrong-peer and cross-key substitution, lock/read denial, egress denial, crash before use, process exhaustion, key destruction, and zero surviving descendant checks. Restore the live witness and submitter to their original unused keys and waiting contexts; if exact restoration is impossible, destroy the entire attempt context and route to Unit 64b rather than substitute identities.
**Output**: Witness/submitter lifecycle and substitution matrix, key-use counters, process/descriptor/network inventories, restoration or purge route, and unchanged identity proof.
**Acceptance**: Live identities/keys remain exact and unused or the whole generation is purged; no signature, submission, provider call, leaked descendant, cross-service capability, or partial replacement passes.

### ⬜ Unit 60f3a: Finalization Attempt Context Convergence Gate
**What**: Perform no launch, mutation, cleanup, ledger, provider, or source call. Validate the exact gap-free receipt chain from Unit 60f3a1, Unit 60f3a2, Unit 60f3a3, Unit 60f3a4, Unit 60f3a5, Unit 60f3a6, Unit 60f3a7, Unit 60f3a8, Unit 60f3a9, Unit 60f3a10, and Unit 60f3a11 against the normative Finalization Attempt Context Protocol. Emit one `FinalizationAttemptContextReadyV1` only when every live service identity, key, journal, policy, route, denial, isolation profile, restart proof, and unused-state inventory is exact.
**Output**: Complete context receipt-chain digest, service/identity/policy inventory, active-platform support proof, and ready or typed failure terminal.
**Acceptance**: The gate is read-only and one-session bounded; no missing, stale, substituted, restored-with-new-identity, partially purged, unsupported, or warning-bearing context proceeds to Unit 60f4.

### ⬜ Unit 60f4: Final Attestor Preflight and Waiting Proof
**What**: Dispatch every final web/native attestor exactly once through its credential-free preflight before any claim. Capture exact repository/ref/workflow SHA, run/attempt/check-run/job IDs, environment, source SHA, nonce, supervisor/policy/public-key/capsule/artifact-attestation digests, active envelope-key events/generations, and complete expected protected-job set. Independently attest Unit 60f3a's promotion-witness and promotion-submitter code/policy/public keys/context, prove both private keys are isolated and unused, and prove the submitter's only network route is the exact operation-scoped `PromotionPackageSubmittedV1` endpoint. Prove every secret-bearing job is waiting for its environment and no provider secret/query has started. Freeze all identities for the generation. Any cancellation, rerun, new attempt, job-set/key-event/witness/submitter drift, bootstrap mismatch, or premature start fails the generation and purges through Unit 64b without claim.
**Output**: Final-attestor preflight manifest; source, witness, and submitter artifact attestations; exact waiting-job/API evidence; source-envelope/witness/submitter generation/nonce bindings; and green or pre-claim abort record.
**Acceptance**: Exactly one preflight per required source succeeds; all bound protected jobs wait, witness and submitter keys remain unused, and no secret work ran. Identities are immutable and consumable by Unit 60f4a and Unit 61; no signer, witness, or submitter key from an earlier renewable-observation run/generation is reused.
**Claim-recovery final-freeze proof**: Re-attest the exact Unit 60f3a `FinalizationClaimRecoveryAuthorityV1` identity/public key/code/policy/journal, root-bound `FinalizationClaimRecoveryPolicyV1`, immutable `ClaimRecoveryObserverGrantV1`, fixed mTLS endpoint, observer-loop identity, zero operational/mutating grants, and typed pre-claim denial in the final preflight manifest. Any identity, policy, journal, observer, endpoint, grant, route, process, or denial drift fails before Unit 61; the failure branch keeps operational methods denied and includes the whole service in Unit 64b cleanup and authoritative nonreachability.
**Pre-registration failure route**: Any non-green/canceled/drifted Unit 60f4 generation proves zero `FinalizationRegistrationPlanV1`, zero `FinalizationRegistrationAttemptV1`, zero registration, and zero claim, then runs Unit 64b's plan-absent cleanup, emits `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, and enters Unit 64a1 with `finalization_generation=null` before loopback. No submission or claim-dependent cleanup exists.

### ⬜ Unit 60f4a: Pre-Claim Proof Freshness Recheck
**What**: Immediately before Unit 61 and after final attestors are waiting, perform only credential-free no-cache GitHub/ref/run/job/artifact-attestation and public runtime-identity liveness checks with fresh GitHub server time. Re-evaluate the already completed Unit 60c59b batch without dispatching any attestor or provider query, and reverify Unit 60f4 source, witness, and promotion-submitter identities remain exact, isolated, unused, and waiting unchanged. If renewable evidence has aged out, or any mismatch/unknown/premature start appears, cancel the frozen jobs, prove authoritative zero `FinalizationRegistrationPlanV1`, zero `FinalizationRegistrationAttemptV1`, zero registration, and zero claim, execute Unit 64b's plan-absent cleanup, emit `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, record the closed generation through Unit 64a1, Unit 64a2, and Unit 64a3 with `finalization_generation=null`, and restart at Unit 60c59b before dispatching an entirely new Unit 60f4 set; never refresh after freeze.
**Output**: Attempt-context digest, exact prior renewable-batch locator/age evaluation, credential-free liveness queries, frozen-attestor waiting proof, predicate inventory, protected records identity, and green or cancel/purge/restart record.
**Acceptance**: Unit 61 starts only from one complete green latest Unit 60c59b pass plus a credential-free liveness recheck bound to Unit 60f3, Unit 60f3a, and Unit 60f4; final jobs still wait exact, witness/submitter keys remain unused, and no source/provider credential or new run is used after freeze. Stale/mismatch/unknown/missing cleanup cannot be waived and creates a new pre-claim generation from Unit 60c59b.
**Claim-recovery freshness proof**: Re-query the frozen claim-recovery process, observer, fixed endpoint, and public artifacts without invoking an operational method; require byte-identical code/policy/public key/journal commitment, exact observer-only capability, zero operational/mutating grants, and typed denial immediately before Unit 61. Any early operational success, disappearance, mutation, or unknown state takes the plan-absent purge branch and cannot be waived.
**Pre-registration failure route**: The cancel/purge/restart branch proves zero `FinalizationRegistrationPlanV1`, zero `FinalizationRegistrationAttemptV1`, zero registration, and zero claim, executes Unit 64b's plan-absent mode, emits `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, and records it through Unit 64a1, Unit 64a2, and Unit 64a3 with `finalization_generation=null`. "Pre-claim" here is temporal only and cannot use the registered-zero-claim terminal.

**Normative Finalization Claim Protocol (reference only)**

The bounded execution units immediately below implement this protocol. This section is reference material, not an executable unit; no actor may perform the whole ceremony in one invocation. Every protocol phrase such as "reconcile without a total retry cap" means a sequence of separately journaled bounded invocations of the named observation unit, never a retry loop inside one unit.

**Split-reference resolution**: In this normative section and earlier contract requirements, "before/start/forbid Unit 61" resolves to the eligibility boundary at Unit 61a plus the final read-only Unit 61 gate. "Unit 61 observes or wakes recovery" resolves only to Unit 61d for reservation loss, Unit 61f for pre-registration close, Unit 61h for an intent-parent close, or Unit 61i for a claim-parent close. "Unit 61 atomic claim handoff" resolves to Unit 61i's exact authority observation. The executable Unit 61 itself never wakes, dispatches, cleans, appends, initializes, or retries.

**Reservation response-loss resolution**: An unchanged exact planned parent is pending, never cleanup-eligible, but it is not a dead end. Unit 61d routes it to Unit 61c1, which may redispatch only the byte-identical deterministic parent-to-attempt CAS under the original idempotency tuple after a fresh strict parent read. Concurrent or delayed identical requests can have only the same attempt child; one wins and every other request reconciles exact equality or CAS loss. Each Unit 61c1 invocation sends at most one request and returns to Unit 61d; there is no in-unit retry loop.
**What**: Dispatch the exact protected coordinator after green Unit 60f4a. First obtain one strict no-cache protected-ledger-ref GET whose canonical body/OID equals the journaled expected parent. Canonicalize `FinalizationRegistrationPlanV1` with that parent, every partial-resource identity, deterministic `FinalizationRegistrationAttemptV1` object bytes/OID, Unit 60f3a cleanup-supervisor identity, the attempt-specific `FinalizationPreRegistrationRecoveryAuthorityV1` graph, and the distinct hosted `FinalizationClaimRecoveryAuthorityV1` service/observer/endpoint tuple plus operational-denial proof; require recovery to fsync the plan before any ledger request. CAS-append the attempt reservation. Reconcile response loss by complete ancestry: exact attempt or authorized descendant means won; only an advanced lineage excluding the attempt OID means lost; unchanged parent remains pending. Exact attempt activates `FinalizationResolverLockV1`, whose only children are `FinalizationRecoveryRegistered` and sibling `FinalizationPreRegistrationCloseStartedV1`. Derive the root-bound 120000ms registration deadline from a strict exact-attempt GET, fsync first-write `FinalizationRegistrationDispatchIntentV1` with canonical registration/close bytes, and start registration within `0..5000` monotonic milliseconds. At/after deadline, pre-registration recovery dispatches/reconciles close-start unless registration is exact. Registration atomically preserves its identity/public key, retires only pre-registration grants, and installs the closed registered grant set; close-start permanently fences registration and leaves pre-registration recovery responsible through supervisor cleanup, signed receipt, exact `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, complete nonreachability including hosted claim recovery, and teardown. After exact registration, CAS-append canonical `FinalizationClaimDispatchIntentV1`; only its confirmed response may obtain exact-intent-head `IntentHeadAnchorV1` satisfying `registrationIssuedAtDateMs <= intentAnchorDateMs <= registrationDeadlineMs-5000` and start claim within `0..5000` monotonic milliseconds. Registered recovery races parent-bound `FinalizationPreClaimCloseStartedV1` against claim intent or claim at/after deadline. Exact close routes through registered cleanup and claim-service purge. Exact `FinalizationClaim` atomically releases the resolver lock and becomes the sole authorization predicate: every registered-recovery method rejects once the validated current head contains that claim in its complete exact operation chain, while the preinstalled claim-recovery methods accept only that claim/operation and later authorized descendants. There is no provider-side route/grant activation transaction. Claim response loss is reconciled by both coordinator and the independent observer's strict ref/commit/tree/blob reads. After exact claim, either actor idempotently initializes the frozen Durable Object operation with `absent_open`, two-phase barrier, coordinator nonce, and exact submitter/validator/relay participants. Claim recovery remains package/candidate-blind and non-publishing; the independent replacement relay alone may append the frozen candidate.
**Resolver-lock enforcement**: The attempt activates one root-bound `FinalizationResolverLockV1` keyed by its deterministic OID. Registration advances that same lock to registered-open, where only exact claim intent or pre-claim close-start is legal. Claim intent advances it to claim-intent-open, where only exact claim or pre-claim close-start is legal. Exact claim or exact authorized close atomically releases the lock. The coordinator and recovery actor test unrelated-append races at both open heads; denial preserves the legal fork, while any impossible observed drift enters root-integrity containment and never becomes a closure cause.
**Waiver guard**: Before registration or dispatch, require every node disposition to be exact `required` with fresh green proof or exact `no_op` with executable impact proof, and require no `ProductChangeReleasedWithWaiver` terminal. Any `deferred`, `waived`, `superseded`, `rolled_back`, or `BLOCKED_HUMAN` node disposition, any malformed `released_with_waiver` node value, or the separate valid `released_with_waiver` Product Change outcome rejects before Durable Object creation, workflow dispatch, or provider mutation.
**Registration-CAS branch**: Before `FinalizationRegistrationPlanV1`, plan-absent cleanup is direct. After a plan but before an exact attempt, cleanup requires a reconciled divergent ledger successor that makes the reservation CAS impossible plus zero `FinalizationRegistrationDispatchIntentV1`; an unchanged parent or ambiguous request remains recovery-owned. Once attempt is exact, global append exclusion makes registration and `FinalizationPreRegistrationCloseStartedV1` its only legal successors. After fsynced dispatch intent, reconcile without a total retry cap until one wins. Exact registration continues with every resource and the exact attempt-specific service identity/public key intact, atomically retires only pre-registration grants, and installs `FinalizationRegisteredRecoveryAuthorityV1` grants. Exact close-start permanently fences registration; Unit 60f3a's attempt-specific recovery authority remains independently restartable, proves zero registration/claim, invokes only Unit 64b's registration-independent cleanup subset via the bound supervisor, reconciles signed durable receipts, appends exact `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, proves complete nonreachability, and retires only then. Lose/delay each plan/reservation/dispatch/registration/close/cleanup/receipt/terminal/proof/teardown request and response, and kill coordinator/recovery/supervisor across every boundary; no unrelated append can strand the reservation, no registration may appear after cleanup begins, and no close-start can remain terminally unfinished.
**Dispatch-intent convergence**: Unit 60f3a's service stores and signs exactly one first-write `FinalizationRegistrationDispatchIntentV1` per attempt. A coordinator proposal that loses that race receives the stored tuple and must adopt it; it cannot register from its stale anchor. Ledger Writer requires that stored digest on both registration and close-start. Response loss re-reads the service journal before any child CAS.
**Reservation-lineage convergence**: The plan binds deterministic attempt object bytes/OID. After a lost reservation response, exact attempt head or any exact authorized descendant containing that OID means the reservation won; Unit 61 adopts registration/close state as observed. Plan-only cleanup requires a protected head advanced from the plan parent whose complete ancestry excludes the attempt OID. Head inequality alone is never enough.
**Reservation-loss recovery convergence**: Once complete ancestry exclusion and zero dispatch intent/registration/claim prove the attempt lost, Unit 61 may only wake recovery and cannot authenticate to cleanup. Unit 60f3a's recovery authority alone invokes the supervisor plan-only subset, reconciles signed WAL/receipts, appends exact `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, proves complete nonreachability, and retires only afterward. Every crash/response-loss boundary remains recovery-owned; supervisor rejection of coordinator credentials is typed/non-consuming, and this path cannot fabricate close-start.
**Registered-recovery convergence**: Registration response loss reconciles to the same attempt-specific identity/public key and one atomic pre-registration-to-registered grant rotation, never a replacement owner. For registered-open or claim-intent-open zero-claim closure, Unit 61 only wakes that `FinalizationRegisteredRecoveryAuthorityV1`; registered recovery alone uses its strict GET, dispatches/reconciles exact parent-bound `FinalizationPreClaimCloseStartedV1`, authenticates to Unit 64b's pre-claim supervisor subset, reconciles its signed request/effect/receipt WAL, appends receipt/nonreachability-bound exact `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, proves complete nonreachability, and tears itself down only afterward. Coordinator credentials are typed/non-consuming rejects throughout, forbidden registered claim-intent/claim/general-ledger calls fail, and crash injection covers every registration, grant-rotation, GET, close-start, wake, supervisor, receipt, append, proof, and teardown boundary.
**Claim-recovery convergence**: The distinct hosted claim-recovery identity remains observer-only through registered-open and claim-intent-open. Exact claim atomically makes registered methods reject and claim-recovery methods authorize from the same ledger state; exact pre-claim close leaves operational denial intact and routes the service to supervisor cleanup. Lost claim response, coordinator death, observer death/restart, or operation-initialization response loss is reconciled from exact claim/journal/operation state without provider-side activation or registered credentials. Every post-claim generic recovery role resolves only to `FinalizationClaimRecoveryAuthorityV1`; wrong-phase credentials and pre-claim operational calls reject typed/non-consuming.
**Replacement relay authority**: Registration freezes an independent `ReplacementRelayAuthorityV1` service identity/code/policy and empty monotonic generation state. After exact claim and only after `dispatch_committed`, `FinalizationClaimRecoveryAuthorityV1` may authorize a higher `ReplacementRelayGenerationV1`; it cannot retrieve package bytes or call exact-candidate Ledger Writer append. The relay alone can retrieve and revalidate the committed candidate and use one generation-bound exact-candidate append token. It has no arbitrary byte/parent, general ledger, withdrawal, abort, or source-rerun route; response loss is observed tokenlessly and later generation preserves the same candidate.
**Pre-claim failure branch**: Treat `FinalizationRecoveryRegistered`, `FinalizationClaimDispatchIntentV1`, `FinalizationPreClaimCloseStartedV1`, and `FinalizationClaim` as separate ledger states under the same resolver lock. A coordinator crash before intent leaves exact registered-open state; after the registration deadline, recovery obtains a fresh strict protected-ledger-ref anchor and races close-start against the sole legal claim-intent CAS from the same exact parent. A crash after intent but before or during claim dispatch leaves exact intent head; claim and close-start again race from that head. Reconcile each response through tokenless no-cache reads without a total retry cap. Exact observed claim continues to Unit 62. Exact winning close-start plus authoritative zero claim selects closure only for `lease_expired_no_intent`, `intent_without_claim`, or `claim_cas_loss`: Unit 61 wakes the same-identity registered recovery, which alone authenticates to Unit 64b's claim-independent cleanup mode; the supervisor cancels and fences every frozen source, validator, dispatch, witness, and submitter job and fsyncs its signed request/effect/receipt WAL; registered recovery reconciles that receipt, revokes registration capability or waits for provider-attested expiry containment, re-queries zero claim, zero Durable Object operation/submission, zero live job/key/context/capability reachability, appends exact `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, and tears itself down only afterward. Unit 61 destroys local coordinator context and ephemeral keys only after issuing the wake and cannot authenticate to cleanup or append the closure. This branch cannot call Unit 64b1, append `FinalizationAborted`, create a Durable Object operation, claim later, or convert unrelated drift into closure.
**Output**: Attempt-specific pre-registration recovery identity/authority/journal; distinct hosted claim-recovery identity, observer grant/loop, fixed endpoint, and operational-denial proof; fsynced `FinalizationRegistrationPlanV1`; on-ledger `FinalizationRegistrationAttemptV1`; optional divergent-parent/plan-only-cleanup record; service-signed first-write `FinalizationRegistrationDispatchIntentV1` plus proposal/equality/response-loss receipt; exact `FinalizationResolverLockV1` attempt/registered-open/claim-intent-open transitions and release; exact registration/pre-registration-close sibling bytes, attempt head, anchor, deadline, request, exclusion, winner, and cleanup-fence records; same-identity/public-key `FinalizationRegisteredRecoveryAuthorityV1` exact grant-rotation and old-grant-denial receipts with no ownerless/dual-grant interval; registered strict-GET, exact parent-bound pre-claim-close-start request/response/reconciliation, Unit 61 wake, typed coordinator-rejection, and forbidden-method receipts; supervisor cleanup request/effect/signed WAL/receipt; exact pre-registration or pre-claim closed append/response reconciliation; exact claim ledger-derived registered-denial/claim-method-authorization proof with zero provider activation calls; observer restart and operation initialization/reconciliation; complete nonreachability and phase-correct recovery retirement proof; exact registration/lease; optional claim intent and regression-safe `IntentHeadAnchorV1`; exact claim or pre-claim-close commits; closure records; coordinator/claim-recovery/reconciler/fallback-reader/replacement-relay identities; mailbox/barrier/relay/validator state only when claimed; and all frozen source/witness/submitter bindings.
**Acceptance**: Proof nodes are shipment-eligible and waiver-free. Registration uncertainty cannot strand resources: before a plan, cleanup is direct; after a plan, exact divergent-parent loss proves registration was never dispatched; after attempt reservation, every unrelated append is denied; one immutable signed dispatch intent defines timing; registration and close-start alone consume the attempt with that digest; and cleanup waits for an exact winner. Delayed registration cannot appear after cleanup. Registration wins preserve the same attempt-specific identity/public key and resolver lock, atomically replace only pre-registration grants with the exact registered GET/pre-claim-close-start/supervisor-cleanup/pre-claim-closed/nonreachability set, expose no ownerless or dual-grant interval, and keep the pre-claim owner restartable. Unit 61 is wake-only after registration; registered recovery is the sole strict-GET/close-start/supervisor/closed caller, coordinator credentials always receive typed non-consuming rejection, and claim-intent/claim/general-ledger attempts are denied. A close-start winner must complete signed-receipt cleanup, exact closed append, dormant claim-recovery nonreachability, and teardown. Exact claim instead atomically retires registered grants and activates the distinct frozen claim-recovery authority with no gap/overlap; that authority can initialize/reconcile the frozen operation after coordinator death but cannot receive bytes, append publication, or call providers directly. Registered-open admits only claim intent or pre-claim close; claim-intent-open admits only claim or pre-claim close; exact claim or close releases the lock. `IntentHeadAnchorV1` obeys both Date bounds and the monotonic dispatch bound. Exact claim alone permits Durable Object operation creation and reaches Unit 62. Every pre-claim closure uses `finalization_generation=null`, proves zero operation/submission/job/key/capability reachability, and forbids claim-dependent mutation. Coordinator/recovery/supervisor crash or response loss converges at every boundary. Caller time, Date regression, renewal, ambiguous response, wrong-phase authority, unrelated work, premature retirement, and unrelated-drift closure fail closed.

### ⬜ Unit 61a: Finalization Eligibility Freeze
**What**: After green Unit 60f4a, perform only the waiver/disposition guard and exact protected-input freeze from the normative Finalization Claim Protocol. Revalidate every required/no-op disposition, absence of any waiver terminal, exact coordinator/recovery/supervisor/observer/relay identities and policies, exact waiting jobs, and the journaled expected protected-ledger parent. Store immutable `FinalizationEligibilityFrozenV1`; do not create a registration plan, append a ledger object, dispatch a service call, or create a Durable Object operation.
**Output**: Exact eligibility/input/identity/policy/parent digest set and `FinalizationEligibilityFrozenV1`, or typed pre-plan rejection.
**Acceptance**: One bounded read-only freeze either succeeds exactly or fails before mutation; no deferred/waived/superseded/rolled-back/blocked disposition, malformed waiver, identity drift, stale parent, or missing denial proof can proceed.

### ⬜ Unit 61b: Registration Plan Fsync
**What**: Consume one exact `FinalizationEligibilityFrozenV1`, canonicalize the deterministic attempt bytes/OID and complete `FinalizationRegistrationPlanV1`, and have only `FinalizationPreRegistrationRecoveryAuthorityV1` fsync the plan to its WAL. End after exact readback. Do not call Ledger Writer, dispatch registration, invoke cleanup, or create an operation.
**Output**: Canonical plan/attempt bytes and OID, fsync/readback receipt, recovery journal locator/digest, and one immutable plan state.
**Acceptance**: The plan binds every partial resource and actor from Unit 61a byte-for-byte; response loss reconciles exact WAL equality; one invocation writes one plan and performs no network mutation.

### ⬜ Unit 61c: Registration Reservation Dispatch
**What**: Consume the exact fsynced plan, obtain one strict no-cache protected-ledger-ref GET matching its parent, journal one reservation request/idempotency tuple, and dispatch at most one CAS append of `FinalizationRegistrationAttemptV1`. End after recording the response union as exact success, explicit CAS loss, timeout, transport error, or response loss. Do not walk ancestry or dispatch registration in this unit.
**Output**: Anchor/body/OID/Date/request-ID evidence, one reservation dispatch journal, response union, and durable `reservation_observation_required` state.
**Acceptance**: At most one reservation CAS is sent; caller time and stale/cached/wrong-parent input fail before dispatch; every non-exact response remains observation-owned without retry.

### ⬜ Unit 61c1: Registration Reservation Exact Redispatch
**What**: Consume Unit 61d's exact `parent_unchanged` result for a response-lost or transport-uncertain reservation. Obtain one fresh strict no-cache protected-ledger-ref GET whose canonical body/OID still equals the fsynced plan parent, journal attempt+1 under the original deterministic attempt bytes/OID and idempotency tuple, and redispatch at most one byte-identical CAS. End after recording its response union and route only to Unit 61d. If the parent changed, send nothing and route that observed lineage to Unit 61d.
**Output**: Prior uncertainty/unchanged-parent binding, fresh anchor, same-bytes/idempotency proof, one optional redispatch response union, and next observation route.
**Acceptance**: At most one identical CAS is sent per invocation; no caller-selected child, new idempotency tuple, cleanup, registration, busy-wait, or in-unit retry exists; delayed/concurrent requests can produce only the deterministic attempt or a reconcilable CAS loss.

### ⬜ Unit 61d: Registration Reservation Observation
**What**: Perform one bounded complete-ancestry observation for Unit 61c or Unit 61c1's deterministic attempt OID. Classify exactly one outcome: `attempt_won`, `parent_unchanged`, `divergent_parent_excludes_attempt`, or `uncertain`. Exact win routes to Unit 61e. Exact divergence plus zero dispatch intent/registration/claim emits one wake to pre-registration recovery and routes to Unit 61k. Exact unchanged parent stores `reservation_redispatch_eligible` and routes to Unit 61c1. Other uncertainty stores `reservation_recovering` and re-enters this same unit only as a later invocation. It performs no CAS, cleanup, registration, or busy-wait loop.
**Output**: Strict ref/commit/ancestry evidence, one reservation outcome, optional recovery wake receipt, and one static route.
**Acceptance**: Head inequality alone never proves loss; unchanged parent can only redispatch exact same bytes through Unit 61c1 and can never clean up; each invocation makes one bounded observation and at most one wake; coordinator credentials never reach cleanup; no in-unit retry or unbounded reconciliation occurs.

### ⬜ Unit 61e: Registration Intent and Dispatch
**What**: From exact `attempt_won`, obtain one strict exact-attempt anchor, derive the root-bound registration deadline, have recovery fsync exactly one first-write `FinalizationRegistrationDispatchIntentV1`, and dispatch at most one registration request within the monotonic bound. Record the response union and end. The stored intent digest is required by registration and close-start; a competing stored tuple is adopted without a second dispatch.
**Output**: Attempt anchor/Date/deadline, signed first-write intent/readback, one registration dispatch record, and durable `registration_observation_required` state.
**Acceptance**: One immutable intent and at most one registration request exist; wrong timing/digest/parent fails before dispatch; no cleanup, grant rotation inference, close append, or response retry occurs in this unit.

### ⬜ Unit 61f: Registration or Pre-Registration Close Observation
**What**: Make one strict protected-ledger/journal observation after Unit 61e and classify exact registration, exact pre-registration close-start, or still-pending/uncertain. Exact registration must prove same identity/public key and atomic installed registered-grant set, then routes to Unit 61g. Exact close-start emits at most one wake to pre-registration recovery and routes to Unit 61k. Pending/uncertain stores `registration_recovering` for a later invocation. Do not invoke supervisor cleanup, append closure, dispatch claim, or loop here.
**Output**: One registration/close outcome, exact grant-rotation proof when registered, optional wake receipt, and one static route.
**Acceptance**: Delayed registration cannot appear after exact close; no ownerless or dual-grant interval passes; one invocation performs one bounded observation and at most one wake; only recovery owns closure work.

### ⬜ Unit 61g: Claim-Intent CAS
**What**: From exact registered-open state, canonicalize and journal one `FinalizationClaimDispatchIntentV1`, dispatch at most one CAS from registered-open, and record its response union. A fresh registered-recovery deadline observation may independently win its parent-bound close-start; coordinator only observes or wakes it. End without obtaining the intent-head timing anchor or dispatching claim.
**Output**: Canonical intent bytes/OID/idempotency, one CAS dispatch/response journal, and durable `claim_intent_observation_required` state.
**Acceptance**: At most one claim-intent CAS is sent; registered-open admits only claim intent or registered-recovery close-start; uncertainty remains observation-owned; coordinator cannot authenticate to registered recovery routes.

### ⬜ Unit 61h: Claim-Intent Outcome and Claim Dispatch
**What**: Perform one bounded strict observation of Unit 61g's parent lineage. On exact intent, obtain one exact-intent-head `IntentHeadAnchorV1`, enforce both Date bounds, journal one claim request, and dispatch at most one `FinalizationClaim` CAS within the monotonic bound. On exact pre-claim close-start, emit at most one wake to registered recovery and route to Unit 61k. On pending/uncertain, store `claim_intent_recovering` for a later invocation. End after one observation plus at most one claim dispatch or one wake; do not reconcile the claim response here.
**Output**: One intent/close outcome, optional timing anchor and claim dispatch journal, optional recovery wake, and one static route.
**Acceptance**: Exact intent alone can dispatch claim; timing bounds are byte-reproducible; at most one claim CAS or one wake occurs; no cleanup, operation creation, or in-unit retry occurs.

### ⬜ Unit 61i: Claim or Pre-Claim Close Observation
**What**: Make one strict tokenless observation of the exact operation chain and actor journals after Unit 61h. Classify exact claim, exact pre-claim close-start with authoritative zero claim, or pending/uncertain. Exact claim must prove atomic registered-method denial and claim-recovery authorization from the same ledger state and routes to Unit 61j. Exact close emits at most one registered-recovery wake and routes to Unit 61k. Pending/uncertain stores `claim_recovering` for a later invocation. Do not initialize an operation or perform closure work here.
**Output**: One claim/close outcome, phase-correct authority proof, optional wake receipt, and one static route.
**Acceptance**: Exact claim and close are mutually exclusive resolver-lock releases; no provider-side grant activation is invented; one invocation performs one bounded observation and at most one wake; wrong-phase credentials reject typed/non-consuming.

### ⬜ Unit 61j: Claimed Operation Initialization
**What**: From Unit 61i's exact claim, dispatch at most one idempotent initialization of the frozen Durable Object operation with `absent_open`, two-phase barrier, coordinator nonce, and exact submitter/validator/relay participants. Reconcile at most one exact status read when the response is not exact, then store either `ClaimedOperationInitializedV1` or durable `operation_initialization_recovering` for a later invocation. Neither coordinator nor claim recovery receives package/candidate bytes or publication authority.
**Output**: Exact claim/participant binding, one initialization request/response, optional one status observation, and initialization terminal or recovering receipt.
**Acceptance**: Only exact claim can create the operation; identical replay is idempotent; mismatch contains; one invocation performs at most one initialization call and one status read; no source/provider/publication work starts.

### ⬜ Unit 61k: Pre-Claim Closure Observation
**What**: After one wake from Unit 61d, Unit 61f, Unit 61h, or Unit 61i, perform one read-only bounded observation of the owning recovery authority, supervisor signed WAL/receipt, exact closed ledger append, complete nonreachability proof, and ordered teardown. Store `preclaim_closure_recovering` while any exact terminal is incomplete or uncertain and re-enter only as a later invocation. Store `FinalizationPreClaimClosureObservedV1` only when the appropriate plan-only, pre-registration-close, or registered-close protocol is fully terminal. Never authenticate as recovery, invoke cleanup, append a terminal, or busy-wait.
**Output**: One closure-state observation, exact signed receipt/closed/nonreachability/teardown bindings when terminal, and recovering or terminal receipt.

Authorization binds the expected root-inventoried Ledger Writer bot ID/login/type, policy-expected `immutable` value, exact preexisting latest response digest or `404`, no discussion side effect, response/timestamp window, and `assets: []`. The annotated tag object/ref chain is the sole target authority; GitHub's response `target_commitish` is provider-informational and cannot override it.

  Persist the exact pre-dispatch request journal before POST, including proof that `target_commitish` is absent, then use a closed response union. `observed_201` records the canonical full `201` response under the pinned media/API version, including `id`, `node_id`, every repository/release/archive URL, `tag_name`, provider-informational `target_commitish`, `name`, `body`, `draft`, `prerelease`, `immutable`, `created_at`, `published_at`, complete `author`, optional discussion field, complete assets, response Date/request ID, and raw-body digest. `lost_response_reconciled` explicitly forbids claiming a POST status/Date/request ID/raw-body digest and instead binds the pre-dispatch journal plus repeated fresh no-cache exact annotated ref/object/peeled chain, release-by-tag, release-by-ID, asset-inventory, and latest-release queries with their own bodies/Date/request IDs; two complete query rounds must be semantically identical. Both outcomes require the same authoritative tag object/ref chain plus exact tag/name/body/flags, expected bot author, expected immutable value, absent/null discussion, empty assets, operation identity, original `created_at` plus the peeled commit/tag date inventory as non-authoritative comparison evidence, `published_at` inside the separately authorized publication window, and latest equal to its exact prior response or still `404`. The response `target_commitish` is retained verbatim but no equality to the publication commit is required and it is never an authorization input. Any query mismatch, or equal body with wrong tag object/ref chain, author/discussion/immutable/latest/timestamp/asset/URL/ID state, contains. Enter `AWAITING_RULESET_POST_OBSERVATION`, revoke or contain the write token, and accept only a fresh separately nonced governance-observer post-snapshot from a new complete three-GET identity/permission/ruleset bundle with identical actor/admin identities, ruleset ETag, and normalized ruleset digest, current signer generation, and valid expiry. Consume that nonce, repeat the complete state query, and append terminal. No Release update/delete/upload or tag update/delete/force route exists.
  Interpret the two Release timestamps disjointly. Preserve `created_at`, compare it with the peeled commit author/committer and annotated-tagger dates, and record GitHub's commit-associated ordering semantics, but do not require equality to those dates or use it to prove the target or POST timing. `published_at` must be non-null and fall inside the bound publication operation window: for `observed_201`, from the fresh pre-dispatch GitHub Date through the `201` response Date with policy skew; for `lost_response_reconciled`, from that same journaled pre-dispatch Date through the first fresh confirming GET Date, and it must be identical across both reconciliation rounds. Reject swapped/equal-by-assumption fields, response-Date substitution, future publication, publication before dispatch, or any outcome authorized by `created_at`. Preserve all original provider values in evidence.
**Output**: Authorization/claim/awaiting-post/terminal-or-containment commits; operation/run/attempt/broker/token identities; signed pre/post snapshot bytes/digests/nonces/expiries and their complete three-response bundle digests with identical ruleset ETag/normalized rules; canonical annotated tag object/ref/name/OID/peeled-publication chain, public tagger/date/message bytes, pre-OID leak-scan digest, object/ref journals, exact-OID retry or unreachable-object disposition, post-create ref-visibility proof, and create receipt; collision/retry/implicit-tag refusal; exact pre-dispatch Release request journal with omitted `target_commitish`; exactly one `observed_201` with original response fields including informational target/Date/request ID/raw digest or `lost_response_reconciled` with two complete repeated no-cache GET evidence sets and no invented POST metadata; Ledger Writer bot identity, timestamp window, latest-before/after state, null/absent discussion, empty assets, IDs/URLs; token teardown; route absence; and authoritative post-query.
**Acceptance**: Guard rejects absent/aborted/stale generations, wrong namespace/object/principal, missing/omitted bypass actors, switched viewer/collaborator, non-built-in admin role, stale/skewed/cached/`304` snapshot evidence, stale/replayed/cross-signed nonce, missing/changing ruleset ETag or normalized digest, permissive/drifting rules, existing mismatch, wrong/personal tagger, noncanonical/leaking message/date, changed orphan OID, an absent/mismatched annotated tag chain before Release POST, or any request `target_commitish`. Exactly Ledger Writer creates the absent canonical nested annotated tag once under active `ProtectedReleaseTagV1`; object/ref split failure either reuses the exact OID or terminates with a preserved-provider record and no Release. The exact closed Release request sets notes/latest false and omits target/discussions. An observed response proves its real `201` metadata while treating response target as informational; a lost response can succeed only through the disjoint two-round full provider-state reconciliation and must not synthesize unavailable POST evidence. Equal body with wrong author/tag-chain/discussion/immutable/latest/timestamp/assets/identity contains; response target alone can neither authorize nor invalidate an otherwise exact chain; nonempty/duplicate assets and every update/delete/force/upload route fail. The body-only empty-assets GitHub Release identifies Unit 64c's publication and Unit 65c's pointer without becoming latest or creating a discussion; matching fresh privileged pre/post bundles and terminal token handling complete before terminal; no private artifact and no alternate shipment authority.
Timestamp acceptance additionally preserves `created_at` beside the exact peeled commit/tag dates without granting it target or operation authority, and proves `published_at` against the actual publication window in both response unions; swapped, conflated, equality-invented, or `created_at`-authorized outcomes contain.

### ⬜ Unit 67: Post-Shipment Read-Only Closeout
**What**: Only after Unit 64c, Unit 65a, Unit 65b, Unit 65c, Unit 65d, and Unit 66 prove effective observed publication and projections, read-only verify ledger/observation/main/tag/Release/providers/feedback/in-flight state. When repair was used, independently re-query `RecoveryUpgradeObservedV1`, its exact `BrokerFoundationV1`, and the full Worker invocation surface to confirm operation and foundation route/dispatch bindings/secrets are absent, foundation storage/runtime/bootstrap identity is restored or deleted, and the repair version is deactivated or expiry-contained and unreachable. Privately enumerate cleanup paths/handles/caches; update Desk/lessons/docs; retire clean task-owned branches/worktrees; notify Slugger; report public-safe inventory.
**Output**: Final evidence index; repair-foundation and broker nonreachability recheck when applicable; Desk completion commit; post-query; worktree cleanup receipt; public-safe inventory; private path-map deletion proof; and Slugger receipt.
**Acceptance**: Guard rejects aborted/non-published generations, missing repair foundation/teardown, or reachable repair runtime/storage/route/bootstrap/secret/capability; zero in-flight operation or residual task branch/worktree; repos clean/synced; unrelated work untouched; no post-shipment corrective mutation.

## Execution

- **TDD strictly enforced**: tests -> red -> implementation -> green -> refactor.
- Commit after each a/b/c phase or atomic live-operation receipt; push every commit.
- Run each repository's focused gate during development and full gate before merge/finalization.
- Before Unit 6c, store every raw transcript and hostile fixture only in private `0600` ephemeral storage and commit no log or transcript. Unit 6c may backfill only scanner-approved public summaries/digests under `./2026-07-20-1958-doing-cross-client-delivery/` after proving repository-history absence, then must purge the retained raw bytes. Later raw evidence remains private ephemeral and is deleted after sanitized proof.
- Before Unit 13b, only the isolated delivery-control-plane mutations explicitly enumerated by Unit 11c1, Unit 12c1, Unit 12c2, Unit 12c3, Unit 12c4, Unit 12c5, Unit 12c6, Unit 12c7, Unit 12c8, Unit 12c9, Unit 12c10, Unit 12c11, Unit 12c12, Unit 12c13, and Unit 12d are allowed. Every secret import in that sequence requires an exact reviewed `BrokerFoundationV1` terminal first; an empty namespace, implicit runtime, or partially created Worker/Durable Object/storage/route/bootstrap identity grants no import authority. Source edits and source/provider-release mutations are forbidden; the active TestFlight task retains exclusive web/native/production Cloudflare/ASC/TestFlight/build/notification/source-worktree and source-owned broker ownership until protected handoff.
- Provider mutations require authority, dry-run/preflight, protected ledger claim, singleton environment approval, per-request drift checks, receipts, terminal/containment, and post-query.
- The first-install bootstrap family (`github-environment-ui-bootstrap-v1` plus same-target-only resume) and restore-only `github-environment-ui-recovery-v1` are the only operations exempt from their target environment's approval gate: all require protected ledger authorization/claim, authenticated actor-ID proof, bounded UI changes, UI/API evidence, and unambiguous terminal state; none can invoke source/provider mutation code. Bootstrap installs a new gate once; recovery requires proven drift and can only restore the last attested policy. All policy changes use the installed gate.
- Cross-provider publication uses exact, age-bounded, source-owned point-in-time attestations plus a durable protected coordinator; it must never claim an atomic distributed snapshot or lock across Cloudflare and ASC because those control planes expose no shared transaction. Later external changes create new state and require a new Product Change.
- Root-policy-governed `ReplayPassGenesisV1` atomically creates/opens the sole first p1 from absent pointer and zero history with `ReplayManifestCursorV1` at the first original ID; subsequent passes use resolved/repair-completed supersession and a fresh first-node cursor. `ReplayNodeIdentityV1` maps each original Unit ID to one executor, but original IDs alone key replay state and cursor. Each `ReplayNodeSetV1` member claims only the exact cursor node through root-duration `ReplayNodeClaimV1` after proving a consumed prefix, unclaimed suffix, and no other claimed/recovering/terminal node. At original Unit 32, Unit 33, Unit 34, and Unit 35 with current-pass retained staged state, green consumption and staged-Worker cleanup activation are exclusive same-parent successors; a cleanup winner must finish node-specific cleanup, authoritative read proof, and cleanup terminal before containment, while a green winner stales only its staged-Worker sibling. Original Unit 34 first proves all prior queue authorities terminal, creates one pass/generation-bound `InstalledQueueRecoveryAuthorityV1`, and leaves it `seeded_pending` on green. A non-green original Unit 35, Unit 36, Unit 51, Unit 37, Unit 37a, Unit 38, or Unit 39 must terminally reconcile that queue before replay failure/checkpoint; device unavailability keeps the cursor recovering and forbids a fresh seed. Original Unit 36 later fences those four Worker activation routes only when current-pass Unit 60c31 retained a fresh available generation, while preserving one dormant original Unit 51 route and the separate queue authority. Its green terminal retires the Worker generation; its non-green terminal carries both Worker and queue contexts through the sole pending bridge, advances only to Unit 51, and leaves later suffix unclaimed. Unit 51 cleanup resolves both contexts before blocking its suffix and failing the pass. Unit 60c39 success alone replays and terminally retires the queue generation. If Unit 60c31 is no-replay against prior terminal Worker retirement, zero retained candidate, and zero current-pass queue, original Unit 36 must also be no-replay and consumes without a fence. Every activated pass, including an all-no-replay pass, traverses its full manifest and Unit 60c59 before resolution. A replay inherits original acceptance; failed or missing proof blocks Unit 60d and Unit 61.
- `LiveStageNodeSetV1` is the exact ordered live sequence Unit 31, Unit 32, Unit 33, Unit 34, Unit 35, Unit 36, Unit 51, Unit 37, Unit 37a, Unit 38, Unit 39, Unit 40, Unit 41, Unit 42, Unit 43, Unit 44, Unit 45, Unit 46, Unit 47, Unit 48, Unit 49, Unit 50, Unit 52, Unit 53, Unit 54, Unit 54a, Unit 55, Unit 56, Unit 57, Unit 58a, Unit 58b, and Unit 59. `LiveStageCheckpointIndexV1` contains only generic-failure causal IDs and therefore excludes Unit 36. Its exact web, native, and safety key sets and values are generated once by Unit 5g23b and reused everywhere; no stage routes backward. Unit 31 retained success freezes candidate-specific `StagedWorkerCleanupAuthorityV1`, a permanent exact-purpose mutation broker, and independent `StagedWorkerReadAuthorityV1`, each with one run-scoped ledger-gated generation containing four causal routes and one dormant Unit 51 route. At Unit 32, Unit 33, Unit 34, and Unit 35, staged-Worker cleanup activation and green terminal/consumption are exclusive successors of that causal node's parent. Cleanup runs mutation and node reconciliation independently; green permanently stales only its staged-Worker sibling. Unit 34 additionally freezes separate `InstalledQueueRecoveryAuthorityV1` before seed; green leaves it `seeded_pending` and executable. The GET-only read authority alone proves exact Worker provider state, and its reconciler's cleanup-terminal CAS retires the Worker generation. Unit 36 later fences the four Worker causal routes only at its legal cursor. Green promotion retires the dormant Worker route but preserves queue authority; a non-green Unit 36 can only append `LiveStageContainmentPendingV1` carrying both contexts, activate Unit 51, and wait. Unit 51 resolves both Worker and queue cleanup before emitting causal Unit 51 `LiveStageFailedV1`; a failure at Unit 35, Unit 37, Unit 37a, Unit 38, or Unit 39 likewise requires queue terminal first. Unit 39 success alone replays and retires the queue generation. No mutation broker may certify its own cleanup, no retained resource or installed queue may lose an executable owner, and no blocked successor may call a provider.
- Finalization registration reservation, registration dispatch, and claim are separate protected transitions under one root-bound `FinalizationResolverLockV1`. Before any ledger call, an attempt-specific independently restartable `FinalizationPreRegistrationRecoveryAuthorityV1` fsyncs `FinalizationRegistrationPlanV1`; the coordinator then CASes on-ledger `FinalizationRegistrationAttemptV1`. An exact divergent successor proves the reservation lost before registration dispatch and permits plan-only cleanup, while an unchanged or ambiguous parent remains recovery-owned. Exact attempt admits only `FinalizationRecoveryRegistered` or sibling `FinalizationPreRegistrationCloseStartedV1`; registered-open admits only `FinalizationClaimDispatchIntentV1` or sibling `FinalizationPreClaimCloseStartedV1`; claim-intent-open admits only exact claim or sibling close-start. A strict attempt-head anchor derives the root-bound lease; fsynced `FinalizationRegistrationDispatchIntentV1` precedes registration. Destructive cleanup after dispatch intent waits for one observed sibling winner. If pre-registration close-start wins with zero registration/claim, the pre-registration authority survives coordinator death through supervisor cleanup, signed receipt reconciliation, exact `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, complete nonreachability, and ordered teardown. If registration wins, it preserves the exact attempt-specific identity/public key and atomically replaces only pre-registration grants with `FinalizationRegisteredRecoveryAuthorityV1` grants. Unit 61 may only wake that identity; after winning pre-claim close-start plus zero claim, registered recovery alone invokes supervisor cleanup, reconciles the signed receipt, appends exact `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, proves complete nonreachability, and tears itself down. Coordinator credentials are typed/non-consuming rejects, recovery cannot clean directly, and the supervisor cannot append. Coordinator, recovery, and supervisor crashes converge at every boundary. Only a confirmed claim-intent response may obtain a strict exact-intent-head anchor satisfying `registrationIssuedAtDateMs <= intentAnchorDateMs <= registrationDeadlineMs-5000`, and that anchor alone launches claim within `0..5000` monotonic milliseconds. Exact claim or exact authorized close releases the lock. Exact claim alone permits Durable Object operation creation; either exact close-start permits only its matching registration-independent or claim-independent cleanup and null-generation closure. Unrelated drift is a root-integrity failure, never a close cause.
- Canonical `FinalizationRegisteredRecoveryPolicyV1` closes the post-registration liveness graph: the same attempt-specific identity receives only strict protected-ledger-ref GET, exact parent-bound `FinalizationPreClaimCloseStartedV1`, claim-independent supervisor cleanup, receipt/nonreachability-bound exact `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, and nonreachability queries. Registered recovery alone owns both Ledger Writer methods and cleanup reconciliation; Unit 61 is wake-only. Coordinator credentials and registered claim-intent, claim, direct-cleanup, package/source/provider, or general-ledger calls are typed/non-consuming rejects. Missing routes, grant overlap/gaps, and teardown before exact closed plus complete nonreachability fail.
- Canonical `FinalizationClaimRecoveryPolicyV1` closes the post-claim liveness graph through a distinct identity frozen dormant before plan. Exact claim atomically retires registered recovery and activates only frozen operation initialization/status, validator/mailbox recovery, next-relay-generation authorization, exact broker-mediated foundation recovery, non-candidate-publishing recovery terminals, and teardown; pre-claim close purges the dormant identity. Claim recovery has no provider credential/direct route, package/candidate bytes, exact-candidate `ReleaseSetPublished` append, source rerun, arbitrary foundation, or general ledger method. Claim-response loss and coordinator death preserve one owner through terminal operation/foundation nonreachability and teardown.
- The pre-registration recovery supervisor-invocation predicate is a closed two-branch union. Before an attempt lands, only complete protected ancestry excluding the deterministic attempt OID plus zero dispatch intent/registration/claim authorizes plan-only cleanup. After an attempt lands, only exact close-start plus zero registration/claim authorizes registration-independent cleanup. Both remain owned after coordinator death through signed cleanup receipt, exact `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, complete nonreachability, and recovery teardown; neither can substitute evidence from the other.
- Every ordinary replay consumption CAS stores immutable `ReplayNodeConsumedV1` with pass/original-node/attempt/decision/result/manifest/pre-post-cursor/transition/ledger bindings. Immediate-poststate equality is sufficient but not necessary: an exact receipt plus a gap-free same-pass same-manifest receipt chain may prove a monotonic descendant current state through later node activity, containment, completion, resolution, or supersession. Recovery never advances a receipted node twice, and pass consumption requires the complete ordered receipt chain.
- Public-safe records are committed on the named iteration branch and merge through protected PR/CI checkpoints after Unit 29e, Unit 42c, Unit 50c, Unit 59c, and Unit 60f3. A generic live failure invokes only its stage's forward checkpoint: web through Unit 42c, native through Unit 50c, or safety through Unit 59c. Finalization consumes only Unit 60f3's exact protected-main commit and GitHub attestation; branch-only records are never authoritative.
- Visual changes or consuming-surface proof require `visual-qa-dogfood` and a closed absurdity ledger.
- Fix ordinary blockers with fresh sub-agents and TDD; surface only true human-only credentials, hardware, or account capability once safe authenticated paths are exhausted.
- Keep checklists and ledger/Desk ownership current after every transition.
